    club_usage_coverage={c:any(float(p.get('minutes') or 0)>0 or bool(p.get('history')) for p in players if p.get('club')==c) for c in club_quality}
    groups=collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in players:groups[(p['club'],p['pos'])].append(p)
    depth={};group_active={};usage_rank={}
    for (club,pos),arr in groups.items():
        ability_order=sorted(arr,key=lambda p:(float(p.get('ca') or 0),float(p.get('pa') or 0)),reverse=True)
        used_order=sorted(arr,key=lambda p:(float(p.get('minutes') or 0),float(p.get('starts') or 0),float(p.get('ca') or 0)),reverse=True)
        group_active[(club,pos)]=used_order[0] if used_order else None
        usage_rank.update({p['id']:i for i,p in enumerate(used_order)})
        for i,p in enumerate(ability_order):
            if pos=='GK':d=[1.0,0.35,0.07][min(i,2)] if i<3 else 0.03
            elif pos=='FWD':d=max(0.18,1.0-0.24*i)
            elif pos=='DEF':d=max(0.28,1.0-0.12*i)
            else:d=max(0.25,1.0-0.12*i)
            depth[p['id']]=d

    raw_scores={}
    for p in players:
        q=_clamp(pct.get(p['id'],0.35));role,attack=_role_profile(p);club=p['club'];games=club_matches.get(club,0);d=depth.get(p['id'],0.5)
        mins=float(p.get('minutes',0) or 0);starts=float(p.get('starts',0) or 0);hist=p.get('history') or []
        # A recent signing should be judged on fixtures he could actually have played.
        first_dates=sorted(str(h.get('date')) for h in hist if h.get('date') and float(h.get('minutes') or 0)>0)
        first_play=first_dates[0] if first_dates else None
        club_dates=sorted(perf[club]['dates'])
        available_games=games;late_arrival=False
        if first_play and club_dates and first_play>club_dates[0]:
            after=sum(1 for x in club_dates if x>=first_play)
            # Only use the late-arrival window when the player has clearly participated
            # in it; this stops pre-transfer league matches making a new signing look unused.
            if after and len(first_dates)>=min(1,after):
                available_games=max(1,after);late_arrival=(available_games<games)
        share=_clamp(mins/(90.0*available_games)) if available_games else 0.0
        start_share=_clamp(starts/available_games) if available_games else 0.0
        active=group_active.get((club,p['pos']));ca=float(p.get('ca') or 100);active_ca=float(active.get('ca') or 100) if active else ca
        _status_text=' '.join(str(p.get(k) or '').lower() for k in ('injury_status','suspension_status','status'))
        current_unavailable=bool(p.get('injured') or p.get('suspended') or 'injur' in _status_text or 'suspend' in _status_text or p.get('injury_return_date') or p.get('injury_evidence'))
        # Missing games are excused only for a ZERO-MINUTE player who has real senior-role
        # evidence. This prevents a low-CA injured reserve being treated as a nailed starter.
        established_absence=bool(current_unavailable and mins<=0 and (q>=0.55 or d>=0.55 or ca>=130))
        absence_excused=established_absence
        expected_exception=established_absence
        if available_games>=1 and club_usage_coverage.get(club,False):
            actual_role=_clamp(0.66*share+0.34*start_share)
            nailed=_clamp(0.86*actual_role+0.14*d)
            if expected_exception:nailed=max(0.68,d*0.80)
        else:nailed=d
        team=_clamp(club_strength.get(club,0.5));pos=p['pos']

        # Add real attacking output to the positional-role estimate without letting a tiny
        # sample dominate.  This separates an attacking wing-back/creator from a CB/DM.
        ga=float(p.get('goals',0) or 0)+float(p.get('assists',0) or 0)
        ga90=(ga*90.0/mins) if mins>=90 else 0.0
        output_weight=_clamp(mins/720.0,0.0,0.42)
        observed_attack=_clamp(ga90/0.62)
        attack=_clamp((1-output_weight)*attack+output_weight*observed_attack)

        if games>=2 and mins==0 and not expected_exception and club_usage_coverage.get(club,False):
            raw=min(4.5,3.5+0.42*q+0.20*d);availability='No minutes · basement price'
        else:
            availability=('Recent arrival · judged since first appearance' if late_arrival else
                          ('Expected role exception' if expected_exception else ('Observed role' if games>=2 and club_usage_coverage.get(club,False) else ('Squad-role projection · player match detail unavailable' if games>=2 else 'Pre-season projection'))))
            if pos=='GK':
                raw=3.70+0.30*q+0.78*nailed+0.82*team;raw=min(5.5,raw)
            elif pos=='DEF':
                # Team quality matters, but attacking threat is required for true premium territory.
                raw=3.60+0.48*q+0.92*nailed+0.78*attack+1.42*team+0.18*(q**4)
            elif pos=='MID' and attack<0.34:
                raw=4.10+0.48*q+1.00*nailed+0.26*attack+0.72*team+0.12*(q**3);raw=min(6.8,raw)
            elif pos=='MID':
                raw=4.10+0.72*q+1.18*nailed+1.70*attack+0.82*team+4.15*(q**4)*(0.30+0.70*nailed)*(0.40+0.60*attack)
                if q>=0.94 and nailed>=0.82 and attack>=0.72 and team>=0.65:raw+=0.8
                raw=min(15.0,raw)
            else:
                raw=4.10+0.76*q+1.28*nailed+1.22*attack+0.92*team+3.85*(q**4)*(0.24+0.76*nailed)
                if q>=0.95 and nailed>=0.82 and team>=0.58:raw+=0.6
                raw=min(14.5,raw)

        # Usage beats reputation once there is evidence, but use AVAILABLE games rather
        # than all club games so a newly-signed starter is not labelled a backup.
        if available_games>=3 and club_usage_coverage.get(club,False) and not expected_exception:
            if share<0.15:
                raw=min(raw,{'GK':4.0,'DEF':4.5,'MID':4.5,'FWD':5.0}[pos]);availability='Rarely used backup'
            elif share<0.35:
                raw=min(raw,{'GK':4.5,'DEF':5.0,'MID':5.2,'FWD':5.5}[pos]);availability='Backup / rotation'
            elif share<0.55:
                raw-=0.35;availability='Rotation / partial starter'

        # League position/performance should materially separate equivalent players.
        if games>=3:
            if pos=='FWD':
                if team<0.28:raw=min(raw,6.0)
                elif team<0.45:raw=min(raw,6.5)
                elif team<0.58:raw=min(raw,7.0)
            elif pos=='DEF':
                if team<0.28:raw=min(raw,4.7)
                elif team<0.42:raw=min(raw,5.2)
            elif pos=='MID' and attack<0.45 and team<0.45:raw=min(raw,5.5)

        raw_scores[p['id']]=raw
        p['price_context']={'quality':round(q,3),'nailedness':round(nailed,3),'minutes_share':round(share,3),
                            'start_share':round(start_share,3),'usage_rank':usage_rank.get(p['id'],0)+1,'role':role,
                            'attack_profile':round(attack,3),'depth_score':round(d,3),'team_strength':round(team,3),
                            'observed_matches':games,'available_matches':available_games,'late_arrival':late_arrival,
                            'first_appearance_date':first_play,'availability_signal':availability,
                            'zero_minute_exception':expected_exception,'ga_per90':round(ga90,3)}

    # v6.1: the raw FM-derived model decides ordering; positional rank then controls
    # the shape of the market. Premium prices are deliberately scarce and the middle/
    # value tiers step down rather than bunching at one high cap.
    ranked={pos:sorted((p for p in players if p['pos']==pos),key=lambda p:raw_scores[p['id']],reverse=True) for pos in ('GK','DEF','MID','FWD')}
    for pos,arr in ranked.items():
        for i,p in enumerate(arr):
            raw=raw_scores[p['id']];c=p['price_context'];att=float(c['at

                elif i==0 and team>=0.70 and att>=0.58:

            elif pos=='MID':

                    # Defensive midfielders are priced for fantasy role, not reputation.
                    if i<8 and q>=0.88 and team>=0.62 and nailed>=0.80:cap=6.0
                    elif i<45:cap=5.5

            obs=max(0,int(c.get('observed_matches') or 0))

            mins=max(0.0,float(p.get('minutes') or 0))
            minute_share=min(1.0,mins/max(1.0,obs*90.0)) if obs else 0.0
            _status_text=' '.join(str(p.get(k) or '').lower() for k in ('injury_status','suspension_status','status'))
            current_unavailable=bool(p.get('injured') or p.get('suspended') or 'injur' in _status_text or 'suspend' in _status_text or p.get('injury_return_date') or p.get('injury_evidence'))

            c['current_unavailable']=current_unavailable

                if minute_share<0.20:

            elif absence_excused:

            c['distribution_cap']=round(raw,2)

    for p in players:
        c=p['price_context'];basement=(c['observed_matches']>=2 and club_usage_coverage.get(p.get('club'),False) and float(p.get('minutes',0) or 0)==0 and not c['zero_minute_exception'])
        base=3.5 if basement else {'GK':4.0,'DEF':4.0,'MID':4.5,'FWD':4.5}[p['pos']]
        price=round(max(base,raw_scores[p['id']])*2)/2
        if basement:price=min(4.5,price)
        p['price']=price;c['price']=price
        c['summary']=(f"{c['role']} · available-minute share {round(c['minutes_share']*100)}% · starts {round(c['start_share']*100)}% · "
                      f"attack {round(c['attack_profile']*100)}/100 · team strength {round(c['team_strength']*100)}/100 · "
                      f"quality {round(c['quality']*100)}/100 · {c['availability_signal']}")


    # v6.5 final launch-price guardrails. Earlier stages build the market shape, but these
    # two cases must never be flattened into ordinary zero-minute backups:
    #   1) established senior players currently unavailable through injury/suspension;
    #   2) strong recent arrivals with too little eligible-club evidence to judge by minutes.
    # This is generic evidence-based logic: no player names or club-specific exceptions.
    _starter_price={}
    for _p in players:
        _c=_p.get('price_context') or {}
        _key=(_p.get('club'),_p.get('pos'))
        if (float(_p.get('minutes') or 0)>=120 and int(_p.get('starts') or 0)>=2) or _c.get('usage_role')=='starter':
            _starter_price[_key]=max(float(_starter_price.get(_key,0) or 0),float(_p.get('price') or 0))

    for _p in players:
        _c=_p.get('price_context') or {};_pos=_p.get('pos')
        if _pos not in ('GK','DEF','MID','FWD'):continue
        _price=float(_p.get('price') or 0);_q=float(_c.get('quality') or 0);_d=float(_c.get('depth_score') or 0);_ca=float(_p.get('ca') or 0)
        _mins=float(_p.get('minutes') or 0);_apps=int(_p.get('apps') or 0)
        _st=' '.join(str(_p.get(k) or '').lower() for k in ('injury_status','suspension_status','status'))
        _unavailable=bool(_p.get('injured') or _p.get('suspended') or 'injur' in _st or 'suspend' in _st or _p.get('return_date') or _p.get('injury_return_date') or _p.get('injury_evidence'))

        # An unavailable established senior option keeps a sensible FPL-like launch floor.
        # Ability/depth only proves they belong in the senior pricing band; it does not make
        # an absent player premium by itself.
        if _mins<=0 and _unavailable and (_q>=0.55 or _d>=0.55 or _ca>=130):
            _floor={'GK':4.5,'DEF':5.0,'MID':5.5,'FWD':6.0}[_pos]
            if _q>=0.80 and _d>=0.70:_floor+=0.5
            _price=max(_price,_floor)
            _c['usage_role']='established_absence'
            _c['availability_signal']='Established senior role · currently unavailable'
            _c['pricing_guardrail']='v65_established_absence'

        # A genuine recent arrival cannot be labelled a £4.5/£5.0 backup from one cameo.
        # Project from quality + depth until there are at least two eligible current-club
        # matches. Keep the projection below a proven starter in the same club/position.
        _avail=int(_c.get('available_matches') or 0);_late=bool(_c.get('late_arrival'))
        if _late and _avail<=1 and _apps<=1 and _q>=0.60 and _d>=0.45:
            if _po

            if _ahead>0:_proj=min(_proj,max({'GK':4.5,'DEF':5.0,'MID':5.5,'FWD':6.0}[_pos],_ahead-0.5))
            _proj=round(_proj*2)/2
            _price=max(_price,_proj)
            _c['usage_role']='new_arrival_projected'
            _c['availability_signal']='Recent arrival · projected from quality and squad role'
            _c['pricing_guardrail']='v65_new_arrival_projection'
            _c['projected_arrival_price']=_proj

        _p['price']=round(_price*2)/2;_c['price']=_p['price']

def scan_completed_results(db:bytes,fixtures:list[dict[str,Any]]):
    keys={(r['home_tid'],r['away_tid'],r['date']):r for r in fixtures}
    teams=sorted({x for r in fixtures for x in (r['home_tid'],r['away_tid'])})
    pat=re.compile(b'|'.join(re.escape(struct.pack('<I',x)) for x in teams))
    found={}
    for m in pat.finditer(db):
        p=m.start()
        if p+86>len(db):continue
        home=u32(db,p); away=u32(db,p+4)
        if away not in teams:continue
        stamp=u16(db,p+8);year=u16(db,p+10);doy=stamp&0x1ff
        if not (1<=doy<=366 and 2024<=year<=2045):continue
        date=fm_date(stamp,year)
        key=(home,away,date)
        if key not in keys:continue
        hs,as_=db[p+84],db[p+85]
        if hs>30 or as_>30:continue
        found[key]=(hs,as_,p)
    # one result per completed fixture; 50 in current regression save
    for key,(hs,as_,p) in found.items():
        f=keys[key];f['status']='played';f['home_score']=hs;f['away_score']=as_;f['result_offset']=p
    return found


def _rich_read_lp_utf8(buf:bytes,pos:int,max_len:int=80):
    if pos+4>len(buf): return None,pos
    n=u32(buf,pos)
    if n>max_len or pos+4+n>len(buf): return None,pos
    raw=buf[pos+4:pos+4+n]
    try:s=raw.decode('utf-8')
    except UnicodeDecodeError:return None,pos
    if any(ord(c)<32 for c in s):return None,pos
    return s,pos+4+n

            _c['availability_signal']='Established senior role · currently unavailable'
            _c['pricing_guardrail']='v65_established_absence'

        # A genuine recent arrival cannot be labelled a £4.5/£5.0 backup from one cameo.
        # Project from quality + depth until there are at least two eligible current-club
        # matches. Keep the projection below a proven starter in the same club/position.
        _avail=int(_c.get('available_matches') or 0);_late=bool(_c.get('late_arrival'))
        if _late and _avail<=1 and _apps<=1 and _q>=0.60 and _d>=0.45:
            if _pos=='GK':_proj=4.0+0.60*_q+0.40*_d
            elif _pos=='DEF':_proj=4.5+0.80*_q+0.50*_d
            elif _pos=='MID':_proj=5.0+1.30*_q+0.60*_d
            else:_proj=5.5+1.40*_q+0.70*_d
            _ahead=float(_starter_price.get((_p.get('club'),_pos),0) or 0)
            if _ahead>0:_proj=min(_proj,max({'GK':4.5,'DEF':5.0,'MID':5.5,'FWD':6.0}[_pos],_ahead-0.5))
            _proj=round(_proj*2)/2
            _price=max(_price,_proj)
            _c['usage_role']='new_arrival_projected'
            _c['availability_signal']='Recent arrival · projected from quality and squad role'
            _c['pricing_guardrail']='v65_new_arrival_projection'
            _c['projected_arrival_price']=_proj

        _p['price']=round(_price*2)/2;_c['price']=_p['price']
        _c['summary']=(f"{_c.get('role','Player')} · available-minute share {round(float(_c.get('minutes_share') or 0)*100)}% · "
                       f"starts {round(float(_c.get('start_share') or 0)*100)}% · attack {round(float(_c.get('attack_profile') or 0)*100)}/100 · "
                       f"team strength {round(float(_c.get('team_strength') or 0)*100)}/100 · quality {round(_q*100)}/100 · {_c.get('availability_signal','')}")

def scan_completed_results(db:bytes,fixtures:list[dict[str,Any]]):
    keys={(r['home_tid'],r['away_tid'],r['date']):r for r in fixtures}
    teams=sorted({x for r in fixtures for x in (r['home_tid'],r['away_tid'])})
    pat=re.compile(b'|'.join(re.escape(struct.pack('<I',x)) for x in teams))
    found={}
    for m in pat.finditer(db):
        p=m.start()
        if p+86>len(db):continue
        home=u32(db,p); away=u32(db,p+4)
        if away not in teams:continue
        stamp=u16(db,p+8);year=u16(db,p+10);doy=stamp&0x1ff
        if not (1<=doy<=366 and 2024<=year<=2045):continue
        date=fm_date(stamp,year)
        key=(home,away,date)
        if key not in keys:continue
        hs,as_=db[p+84],db[p+85]
        if hs>30 or as_>30:continue
        found[key]=(hs,as_,p)
    # one result per completed fixture; 50 in current regression save
    for key,(hs,as_,p) in found.items():
        f=keys[key];f['status']='played';f['home_score']=hs;f['away_score']=as_;f['result_offset']=p
    return found


def _rich_read_lp_utf8(buf:bytes,pos:int,max_len:int=80):
    if pos+4>len(buf): return None,pos
    n=u32(buf,pos)
    if n>max_len or pos+4+n>len(buf): return None,pos
    raw=buf[pos+4:pos+4+n]
    try:s=raw.decode('utf-8')
    except UnicodeDecodeError:return None,pos
    if any(ord(c)<32 for c in s):return None,pos
    return s,pos+4+n

        'blocks':c[55],'assists':c[56],
        'yellow_cards':c[63],'red_cards':c[64],'sub_off':c[67],'sub_on':c[71],
        'sent_off_minute':c[74],'match_position_marker':c[77],

        'headers_attempted':c[95],'headers_won':c[96],

        'team_goals_while_on_pitch':c[145],'team_goals_conceded_while_

function fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,club,date){return (payload.fixtures||[]).filter(f=>f.status==='played'&&String(f.date||'')>String(date||'')&&(f.home===club||f.away===club)).length}
function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}
function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}

function newsRow(item,type){if(type==='price')return `<div class="newsRow" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span><b>£${Number(item.old_price).toFixed(1)}</b> → <b class="${item.new_price>item.old_price?'newsPriceUp':'newsPriceDown'}">£${Number(item.new_price).toFixed(1)}</b></span></div>`;if(type==='transfer')return `<div class="newsRow transfer" data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.old_club||''} ${item.new_club||item.club||''} ${item.pos||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.old_club||item.from||'Outside league')}</span><span>${esc(item.new_club||item.to||item.club||'')}</span><span>${esc(item.value||'—')}</span><span>${esc(item.pos||'')}</span><span>£${Number(item.price||0).toFixed(1)}m</span></div>`;return `<div class="newsRow status" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos} ${item.detail||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span>${esc(item.detail||'Update')}</span></div>`}
function renderNewsCard(id,title,items,type){const el=$(id);if(!el)return;items=items||[];const icons={newsTransfers:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 8h13m0 0-3-3m3 3-3 3M19 16H6m0 0 3 3m-3-3 3-3"/></svg>',newsPriceUp:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 17 6-6 4 4 6-7m-5 0h5v5"/></svg>',newsPriceDown:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 7 6 6 4-4 6 7m-5 0h5v-5"/></svg>',newsInjuries:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="5" y="6" width="14" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M9 6V4h6v2m-3 4v6m-3-3h6"/></svg>',newsSuspensions:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="m6 6 12 12"/></svg>'},preview=items.slice(0,5),rest=items.slice(5),key=id+'Full',head=type==='transfer'&&items.length?'<div class="newsTransferHead"><span>Player</span><span>Old club</span><span>New club</span><span>FM value</span><span>Pos</span><span>FPL price</span></div>':'',activeLabel=type==='status'?'Active now':'Latest import',emptyText=type==='status'?'No active players.':'No changes this import.',search=items.length?`<input class="ctrl newsSearch" data-news-search="${id}" placeholder="Search player or club…">`:'',rows=preview.length?preview.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join(''):`<div class="newsEmpty"><span class="newsEmptyIcon">${type==='status'?'＋':'•'}</span><span>${emptyText}</span></div>`,more=rest.length?`<div id="${key}" class="newsHidden">${rest.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join('')}</div><button class="btn dark newsMore" data-news-toggle="${key}" data-news-count="${items.length}">View all ${items.length}</button>`:'';el.innerHTML=`<div class="newsHead"><div class="newsTitleWrap"><span class="newsIcon">${icons[id]||icons.newsTransfers}</span><div><h3 style="margin:0">${esc(title)}</h3><div class="muted">${activeLabel}${type==='transfer'?' · senior/first-team only':''}</div></div></div><div class="newsCount">${items.length}</div></div><div class="newsRows">${search}${head}${rows}${more}</div>`}
function filterNewsCard(id,q){const el=$(id);if(!el)return;const term=String(q||'').trim().toLowerCase(),rows=[...el.querySelectorAll('[data-news-text]')],full=$(id+'Full'),toggle=el.querySelector('[data-news-toggle]');if(term){if(full)full.classList.remove('newsHidden');rows.forEach(r=>r.style.display=(r.dataset.newsText||'').includes(term)?'':'none');if(toggle)toggle.style.display='none'}else{rows.forEach(r=>r.style.display='');if(full)full.classList.add('newsHidden');if(toggle){toggle.style.display='';toggle.textContent=`View all ${toggle.dataset.newsCount}`}}}
function renderNews(){const _newsCleared=(()=>{try{return localStorage.getItem('fmFantasyCloudDatabaseCleared')==='1'||sessionStorage.getItem('fmFantasyCloudDatabaseCleared')==='1'}catch(_){return false}})();const n=_newsCleared?null:state.news;if($('newsStamp'))$('newsStamp').textContent=n?(n.initial?`Database imported · active injuries/suspensions shown · transfer and price-change news begins after the first Gameweek update.`:`Latest Gameweek import · GW ${n.gameweek||'—'} · ${new Date(n.generatedAt).toLocaleString('en-GB')}`):'Season import establishes the database. Active injuries/suspensions will appear immediately; other news starts after the first Gameweek update.';renderNewsCard('newsTransfers','Transfers',n?.transfers,'transfer');renderNewsCard('newsPriceUp','Price increases',n?.priceIncreases,'price');renderNewsCard('newsPriceDown','Price decreases',n?.priceDecreases,'price');

 payload.meta.price_policy='launch price frozen at database import; only post-import sustained form can move price';payload.meta.price_merge_policy='v66 corrective model guardrails rebaseline before dynamic pricing';return{increases:inc.sort((a,b)=>b.delta-a.delta),decreases:dec.sort((a,b)=>a.delta-b.delta)} }
function fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload){const ids=new Set();const byClub=new Map();for(const p of payload?.players||[]){if(p.available===false||p.visible===false)continue;const arr=byClub.get(p.club)||[];arr.push(p);byClub.set(p.club,arr);if((p.history||[]).length||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||'')))ids.add(String(p.pid))}for(const arr of byClub.values()){arr.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.ca||0)-Number(a.ca||0));for(const p of arr.slice(0,18))ids.add(String(p.pid))}return ids}
function fmTransferValue(p){const v=p?.market_value??p?.transfer_value??p?.fm_value??p?.value;if(v===undefined||v===null||v==='')return '—';if(typeof v==='number'){if(v>=1000000)return `£${(v/1000000).toFixed(v>=10000000?1:2)}m`;if(v>=1000)return `£${Math.round(v/1000)}k`;return `£${v.toLocaleString('en-GB')}`}return String(v)}
function fmFmtStatusDate(v){if(!v)return'';try{const d=new Date(String(v).length<=10?String(v)+'T12:00:00':v);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return String(v);return d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:d.getFullYear()===new Date().getFullYear()?undefined:'numeric'})}catch(_e){return String(v)}}
function fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,club,date){return (payload.fixtures||[]).filter(f=>f.status==='played'&&String(f.date||'')>String(date||'')&&(f.home===club||f.away===club)).length}
function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}
function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}
function fmBuildInitialNews(payload){const active={injuries:fmInferActiveInjuries(payload),suspensions:fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload)};state.activeStatuses=active;return{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),gameweek:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0),initial:true,transfers:[],priceIncreases:[],priceDecreases:[],injuries:active.injuries,suspensions:active.suspensions}}

function fmBuildImportNews(changes,priceMoves,payload,oldP){const oldMap=new Map((oldP?.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),newMap=new Map((payload.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),seniorNew=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload),seniorOld=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(oldP||{});const transfers=[],seenTransfers=new Set();const seniorEvidence=p=>{if(!p)return false;const ctx=p.price_context||{},played=Number(p.minutes||0)>0||(p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),rank=Number(ctx.usage_rank||99),quality=Number(ctx.quality||0),nailed=Number(ctx.nailedness||0);return played||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||''))||(rank<=18&&quality>=.48&&nailed>=.35)};const addTransfer=(x,p,from,to,kind='move')=>{if(!p)return;const id=String(p.pid);if(!seniorNew.has(id)&&!seniorOld.has(id))return;if(!seniorEvidence(p))return;if(kind==='added'&&!((p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0)||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||((p.price_context?.usage_rank||99)<=16&&Number(p.price_context?.quality||0)>=.62&&Number(p.price_context?.nailedness||0)>=.48)))return;const k=`${id}|${from}|${to}`;if(seenTransfers.has(k))return;seenTransfers.add(k);transfers.push({name:x?.name||playerName(p),old_club:from||'Outside league',new_club:to||p.club||'',club:to||p.club||'',pos:p.pos||'',value:fmTransferValue(p),price:Number(p.price||0)})};for(const x of changes?.players_moved||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,x.from,x.to||p?.club,'move')}for(const x of changes?.players_added||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,'Outside league',p?.club,'added')}const active={injuries:fmInferActiveInjuries(payload),suspensions:fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload)};state.activeStatuses=active;return{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),gameweek:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0),transfers,priceIncreases:priceMoves?.increases||[],priceDecreases:priceMoves?.decreases||[],injuries:active.injuries,suspensions:active.suspensions}}

function fmCleanNamePart(x){return String(x||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}
function fmCanonicalPlayerName(p){
 if(!p)return 'Unknown';
 const preferred=fmCleanNamePart(p.preferred_name||p.known_as||p.common_name||p.nickname);
 const surname=fmCleanNamePart(p.football_surname||p.surname||p.family_name||p.last_name);
 const explicit=fmCleanNamePart(p.canonical_display_name||p.public_name||p.football_display_name);
 if(explicit&&(!preferred||explicit.toLowerCase()!==preferred.toLowerCase()))return explicit;
 if(preferred){
   if(!surname||preferred.toLowerCase()===surname.toLowerCase()||preferred.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).includes(surname.toLowerCase()))return preferred;
   if(preferred.split(/\s+/).length>1)return preferred;
   return `${preferred} ${surname}`;
 }
 const display=fmCleanNamePart(p.display_name);
 if(display)return display;
 const first=fmCleanNamePart(p.first_name||p.forename), normalSurname=surname;
 if(first&&normalSurname)return `${first} ${normalSurname}`;
 return fmCleanNamePart(p.name||p.legal_full_name)||'Unknown';
}

function playerGWState(p,gw){const fs=fixturesForClub(p.club,gw),played=fs.filter(f=>f.status==='played'),future=fs.filter(f=>f.status!=='played'),raw=latestPoints(p,gw),mins=playerMinutesGW(p.id,gw),rows=(p.history||[]).filter(h=>Number(h.gameweek)===Number(gw));return{fs,played,future,raw,mins,hasDetail:rows.length>0,appeared:mins>0}}

function filteredPlayers(){const q=$('search').value.trim().toLowerCase(),club=$('clubFilter').value,pos=$('posFilter').value,sort=$('sortFilter').value;let arr=PLAYERS.filter(p=>p.visible&&p.available!==false&&(!q||playerName(p).toLowerCase().includes(q)||String(p.club||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))&&(!club||p.club===club)&&(!pos||p.pos===pos));const cmp={price_desc:(a,b)=>b.price-a.price,price_asc:(a,b)=>a.price-b.price,points_desc:(a,b)=>b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points,points_asc:(a,b)=>a.fantasy_points-b.fantasy_points,form_desc:(a,b)=>Number(b.form_points||b.form||0)-Number(a.form_points||a.form||0),form_asc:(a,b)=>Number(a.form_points||a.form||0)-Number(b.form_points||b.form||0),rating_desc:(a,b)=>Number(b.avg_rating||0)-Number(a.avg_rating||0),rating_asc:(a,b)=>Number(a.avg_rating||0)-Number(b.avg_rating||0),name_asc:(a,b)=>playerName(a).localeCompare(playerName(b)),name_desc:(a,b)=>playerName(b).localeCompare(playerName(a))};arr.sort(cmp[sort]||cmp.points_desc);return arr}

function minimumCost(slots,used,clubs){let sum=0;const u=new Set(used);for(const pos of slots){const p=PLAYERS.filter(x=>x.visible&&x.available!==false&&x.pos===pos&&!u.has(x.id)&&(clubs[x.club]||0)<3).sort((a,b)=>a.price-b.price)[0];if(!p)return Infinity;sum+=p.price;u.add(p.id);clubs[p.club]=(clubs[p.club]||0)+1}return sum}
function autoFill(){const v=countSquad(),need={GK:2-v.c.GK,DEF:5-v.c.DEF,MID:5-v.c.MID,FWD:3-v.c.FWD};if(Object.values(need).some(n=>n<0))return alert('Remove the player causing the positional quota issue first.');let slots=[];for(const pos of ['GK','DEF','MID','FWD'])for(let i=0;i<need[pos];i++)slots.push(pos);if(!slots.length)return;const base=[...state.squad],score=p=>(p.fantasy_points*1.25+p.avg_rating*4)/(p.price||1),budget=Number(state.bank||0);const cand={};for(const pos of ['GK','DEF','MID','FWD'])cand[pos]=PLAYERS.filter(p=>p.visible&&p.available!==false&&p.pos===pos&&!base.includes(p.id)).sort((a,b)=>score(b)-score(a));let answer=null,nodes=0;function dfs(i,chosen,clubs,cost){if(answer||nodes++>350000)return;if(i===slots.length){answer={ids:[...chosen],cost};return}const pos=slots[i];for(const p of cand[pos]){if(chosen.includes(p.id)||(clubs[p.club]||0)>=3||cost+p.price>budget+1e-9)continue;const nc={...clubs,[p.club]:(clubs[p.club]||0)+1},ch=[...chosen,p.id],ns=cost+p.price;const rem=slots.slice(i+1);const min=minimumCost(rem,ch,{...nc});if(ns+min>budget+1e-9)continue;dfs(i+1,ch,nc,ns);if(answer)return}}dfs(0,base,{...v.clubs},0);if(!answer)return alert('Auto Fill could not complete this draft with the money currently in your bank.');state.squad=answer.ids;fmRecalculateBank();if(validateSquad().ok)autoXI();save();renderAll()}
function xiCounts(ids){const c={GK:0,DEF:0,MID:0,FWD:0};ids.map(pby).filter(Boolean).forEach(p=>c[p.pos]++);return c}
function legalXI(ids){const c=xiCounts(ids);return ids.length===11&&c.GK===1&&c.DEF>=3&&c.DEF<=5&&c.MID>=2&&c.MID<=5&&c.FWD>=1&&c.FWD<=3}
function autoXI(){if(!validateSquad().ok)return;const all=squad();let best=null;for(let d=3;d<=5;d++)for(let m=2;m<=5;m++){const f=10-d-m;if(f<1||f>3)continue;const arr=[...all.filter(p=>p.pos==='GK').sort((a,b)=>b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points).slice(0,1),...all.filter(p=>p.pos==='DEF').sort((a,b)=>b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points).slice(0,d),...all.filter(p=>p.pos==='MID').sort((a,b)=>b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points).slice(0,m),...all.filter(p=>p.pos==='FWD').sort((a,b)=>b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points).slice(0,f)];if(arr.length!==11)continue;const pts=arr.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.fantasy_points,0);if(!best||pts>best.pts)best={pts,arr}}if(best){state.starters=best.arr.map(p=>p.id);const used=new Set(state.starters);state.bench=all.filter(p=>!used.has(p.id)).sort((a,b)=>a.pos==='GK'?-1:b.pos==='GK'?1:b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points).map(p=>p.id);if(!state.captain||!state.starters.includes(state.captain))state.captain=[...state.starters].sort((a,b)=>(pby(b)?.fantasy_points||0)-(pby(a)?.fantasy_points||0))[0]||null;if(!state.vice||!state.starters.includes(state.vice)||state.vice===state.captain)state.vice=state.starters.find(id=>id!==state.captain)||null}}
function reconcileXI(){if(!validateSquad().ok){state.starters=[];state.bench=[];return}if(legalXI(state.starters)&&new Set([...state.starters,...state.bench]).size===15)return;autoXI()}
function formation(ids=state.starters){const c=xiCounts(ids);return ids.length===11?`${c.DEF}-${c.MID}-${c.FWD}`:'—'}

function fmPlayerOverviewStats(p){
 const hist=p?.history||[], uniq=new Map();
 for(const h of hist){const k=`${h.match_id??''}|${h.date??''}|${h.home??''}|${h.away??''}`;if(!uniq.has(k))uniq.set(k,h)}
 const hs=[...uniq.values()];

 return {
  apps:Math.max(Number(p?.apps||0),hs.filter(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0).length),
  minutes:Math.max(Number(p?.minutes||0),sum('minutes')),
  goals:Math.max(Number(p?.goals||0),sum('goals')),
  assists:Math.max(Number(p?.assists||0),sum('assists')),
  points:Math.max(Number(p?.fantasy_points||0),sum('fpl_points')),
  rating:Number(p?.avg_rating||0)||(ratings.length?ratings.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/ratings.length:0)
 }
}

function fmApplyPostPayloadPricingCorrections(payload){
 payload=payload||{};payload.meta=payload.meta||{};let changed=0;
 for(const p of payload.players||[]){
  const c=p.price_context||(p.price_context={});const pos=String(p.pos||'');if(!['GK','DEF','MID','FWD'].includes(pos))continue;
  let price=Number(p.price||0),q=Number(c.quality||0),d=Number(c.depth_score||0),ca=Number(p.ca||0),mins=Number(p.minutes||0),apps=Number(p.apps||0);
  const st=[p.injury_status,p.suspension_status,p.status].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase();
  const unavailable=!!(p.injured||p.suspended||st.includes('injur')||st.includes('suspend')||p.return_date||p.injury_return_date||p.injury_evidence);
  if(mins<=0&&unavailable&&(q>=0.55||d>=0.55||ca>=130)){
   let floor={GK:4.5,DEF:5.0,MID:5.5,FWD:6.0}[pos];if(q>=0.80&&d>=0.70)floor+=0.5;
   if(price<floor){price=floor;changed++}
   c.usage_role='established_absence';c.availability_signal='Established senior role · currently unavailable';c.pricing_guardrail='v67_postpayload_established_absence';
  }
  const avail=Number(c.available_matches||0),late=!!c.late_arrival;
  if(late&&avail<=1&&apps<=1&&q>=0.60&&d>=0.45){
   let proj=pos==='GK'?4.0+0.60*q+0.40*d:pos==='DEF'?4.5+0.80*q+0.50*d:pos==='MID'?5.0+1.30*q+0.60*d:5.5+1.40*q+0.70*d;
   proj=Math.round(proj*2)/2;
   if(price<proj){price=proj;changed++}
   c.usage_role='new_arrival_projected';c.availability_signal='Recent arrival · projected from quality and squad role';c.pricing_guardrail='v67_postpayload_new_arrival_projection';c.projected_arrival_price=proj;
  }
  if(c.pricing_guardrail){p.price=price;p.model_price=price;c.price=price;}
 }
 payload.meta.postpayload_pricing_corrections=changed;payload.meta.price_merge_policy='v67 post-payload corrective pricing before launch freeze/dynamic merge';return payload;
}

function fmPriceSample(h){const mins=Number(h?.minutes||0),pts=Number(h?.fpl_points||0),ga=Number(h?.goals||0)+Number(h?.assists||0),rating=Number(h?.rating||0);if(mins<=0)return null;if(ga>0||pts>=7||rating>=7.4)return 1;if(mins>=60&&ga===0&&pts<=2&&(rating===0||rating<=6.6))return -1;return 0}
function fmApplyDynamicPricing(oldP,payload,changes){const oldMap=new Map((oldP.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),inc=[],dec=[];for(const p of payload.players||[]){const o=oldMap.get(String(p.pid));p.model_price=Number(p.price||0);if(!o){p.launch_price=Number(p.price||0);p.dynamic_price=Number(p.price||0);p.price_baseline_gw=Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0);p.price_change_history=[];p.price_tracker=fmPriceTrackerSeed(p,p.price_baseline_gw);continue}

 // v6.6: model-correction guardrails must be allowed to replace a broken frozen launch
 // price. v6.5 correctly calculated Tosin/Gimenez-style prices, but this update path then
 // restored the old baseline. A guardrail is an importer correction, not a weekly market
 // move, so re-baseline immediately and do not create a price-rise/fall news event.

   p.launch_price=correctedModelPrice;p.dynamic_price=correctedModelPrice;p.price=correctedModelPrice;
   p.price_baseline_gw=Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0);p.price_change_history=[];
   p.price_tracker=fmPriceTrackerSeed(p,p.price_baseline_gw);p.price_context=p.price_context||{};
   p.price_context.price=correctedModelPrice;p.price_context.rebaseline_applied='v66_corrective_model_guardrail';continue
 }

function fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,club,date){return (payload.fixtures||[]).filter(f=>f.status==='played'&&String(f.date||'')>String(date||'')&&(f.home===club||f.away===club)).length}
function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}
function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}

function fmBuildImportNews(changes,priceMoves,payload,oldP){const oldMap=new Map((oldP?.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),newMap=new Map((payload.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),seniorNew=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload),seniorOld=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(oldP||{});const transfers=[],seenTransfers=new Set();const seniorEvidence=p=>{if(!p)return false;const ctx=p.price_context||{},played=Number(p.minutes||0)>0||(p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),rank=Number(ctx.usage_rank||99),quality=Number(ctx.quality||0),nailed=Number(ctx.nailedness||0);return played||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||''))||(rank<=18&&quality>=.48&&nailed>=.35)};const addTransfer=(x,p,from,to,kind='move')=>{if(!p)return;const id=String(p.pid);if(!seniorNew.has(id)&&!seniorOld.has(id))return;if(!seniorEvidence(p))return;if(kind==='added'&&!((p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0)||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||((p.price_context?.usage_rank||99)<=16&&Number(p.price_context?.quality||0)>=.62&&Number(p.price_context?.nailedness||0)>=.48)))return;const k=`${id}|${from}|${to}`;if(seenTransfers.has(k))return;seenTransfers.add(k);transfers.push({name:x?.name||playerName(p),old_club:from||'Outside league',new_club:to||p.club||'',club:to||p.club||'',pos:p.pos||'',value:fmTransferValue(p),price:Number(p.price||0)})};for(const x of changes?.players_moved||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,x.from,x.to||p?.club,'move')}for(const x of changes?.players_added||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,'Outside league',p?.club,'added')}const active={injuries:fmInferActiveInjuries(payload),suspensions:fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload)};state.activeStatuses=active;return{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),gameweek:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0),transfers,priceIncreases:priceMoves?.increases||[],priceDecreases:priceMoves?.decreases||[],injuries:active.injuries,suspensions:active.suspensions}}
